[basemain_pkd.py] class PvtV2B4_FPN_ZoomNeXt_PKD_SKD(
    PKDSKDTrainingMixin,
    PvtV2B4_FPN_ZoomNeXt,
):
    """ZoomNeXt with PKD/SKD used only by the training-time objective.

    Main prediction:
        1.0x + 1.5x -> MHSIU2 -> FPN/RGPU -> logits.
    SKD prediction when ``skd_view='image_s'``:
        0.5x + generated 0.75x -> the same network -> logits.

    Evaluation remains the unmodified ZoomNeXt inference path.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        pkd_weight=0.5,
        skd_weight=1.0,
        pkd_erode_kernel=5,
        pkd_min_valid_pixels=16,
        skd_view="image_s",
        skd_ramp_start=0.0,
        skd_ramp_end=0.0,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__(**kwargs)
        self._init_pkd_skd(
            pkd_weight=pkd_weight,
            skd_weight=skd_weight,
            pkd_erode_kernel=pkd_erode_kernel,
            pkd_min_valid_pixels=pkd_min_valid_pixels,
            skd_view=skd_view,
            skd_ramp_start=skd_ramp_start,
            skd_ramp_end=skd_ramp_end,
        )
